# MAUI Proven Memory Leak Catalog

Last updated: 2026-06-28

## Catalog (247 Proven Or Upstream-Tracked Leaks)

| ID | Leak short description | Plain-language summary | Affected platform(s) | Retention targets / proof signal | Retention behavior | Trigger likelihood | Proof repro | Filed MAUI issue |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| C001 | `WebView.Source` retains `WebView` through shared `WebViewSource.SourceChanged` | Occurs when `WebView` uses shared `WebViewSource.SourceChanged` that outlives it. Retains `WebView` until the long-lived source is cleared/replaced or the subscriber unsubscribes. Work around by clearing that property/source, unsubscribing, or avoiding long-lived shared publishers before disposal/navigation. | Cross-platform/Core | Retains WebView | Unbounded per subscribed control/page while the shared publisher lives. | Specific feature path; common in apps using the affected web/auth control or option. | [repro/webviewsource-leak](https://github.com/AdamEssenmacher/maui/tree/repro/webviewsource-leak) | [#35483](https://github.com/dotnet/maui/issues/35483) |
| C002 | `SelectionList` retains `SelectableItemsView` through external `SelectedItems.CollectionChanged` | Occurs when `SelectableItemsView` uses external `SelectedItems.CollectionChanged` that outlives it. Retains `SelectableItemsView` until the long-lived source is cleared/replaced or the subscriber unsubscribes. Work around by clearing that property/source, unsubscribing, or avoiding long-lived shared publishers before disposal/navigation. | Cross-platform/Core | Retains SelectableItemsView | Unbounded per subscribed control/page while the shared publisher lives. | Specific usage path documented by the repro. | [repro/selecteditems-selectionlist-leak](https://github.com/AdamEssenmacher/maui/tree/repro/selecteditems-selectionlist-leak) | [#35497](https://github.com/dotnet/maui/issues/35497) |
| C003 | iOS `CarouselViewController2` orientation notification observer is not stored/disposed | Occurs when iOS `CarouselViewController2` orientation notification observer is not stored/disposed. Retains `CarouselViewController2` observer/controller graphs until the observer, event, trigger, or listener is detached/cleared. Work around by detaching triggers, handlers, observers, or listeners before replacement/navigation when possible. | iOS/Mac Catalyst | iOS CarouselViewController2 orientation notification observer is not stored/disposed | Unbounded per disconnected/replaced native peer or handler while the retaining native/app root survives. | Regular in dynamic UIs that replace templates/content/header/footer/items or disconnect handlers. | [repro/carousel-view-2-observer-leak](https://github.com/AdamEssenmacher/maui/tree/repro/carousel-view-2-observer-leak) | [#35472](https://github.com/dotnet/maui/issues/35472) |

| M050 | Android ActivityStateManager duplicates lifecycle callbacks | Occurs when Android activities are recreated and ActivityStateManager does not unregister old lifecycle callbacks. Retains lifecycle callback targets and multiplies Platform.ActivityStateChanged delivery until process restart. Work around by minimizing activity recreation and avoiding long-lived subscribers until the manager cleanup is fixed. | Android | Activity lifecycle callbacks, ActivityStateChanged subscribers, app lifecycle state | Unbounded across Activity recreation cycles | Typical under rotation, process recreation, and multi-activity lifecycle churn | [#36035](https://github.com/dotnet/maui/issues/36035) | [#36035](https://github.com/dotnet/maui/issues/36035) |
| M051 | `HideSoftInputOnTapped` can retain stale ShellContent pages when `NavigatedFrom` does not fire | Occurs when a templated ShellContent page with `HideSoftInputOnTapped="True"` is hidden and replaced by absolute Shell navigation without `NavigatedFrom`. The scoped `HideSoftInputOnTappedChangedManager` keeps the page in `_contentPages`, so the old page graph remains alive and the global tap tracker stays enabled for later pages. Work around by avoiding the property on routes that can be hidden/replaced this way, or clearing the flag before hiding/navigating. | Android | Stale `ContentPage`, ShellContent page graph, `HideSoftInputOnTappedChangedManager._contentPages`, global `TapWindowTracker` behavior | App/window-scope lifetime while the manager remains alive and the stale page stays in `_contentPages` | Specific Shell templated-content and absolute-navigation path, but realistic for login-to-home route replacement flows | [#35890](https://github.com/dotnet/maui/issues/35890) | [#35890](https://github.com/dotnet/maui/issues/35890) |
| M052 | Windows WebView2 rapid create/dispose leaks memory and can throw from stale WebView2 event handling | Occurs when a Windows MAUI `WebView` backed by WebView2 is repeatedly created, added, removed, and disposed in tight loops. The upstream repro reports increasing process memory and `NullReferenceException` from WebView2 event handling during rapid disposal. Work around by avoiding rapid churn where possible, reusing WebView containers, and allowing wider teardown delays until the WebView2 lifecycle cleanup is fixed. | Windows | WebView2-backed MAUI `WebView`, WebView2 event/lifecycle state, page/container graph, process memory | Unbounded process memory growth across repeated WebView container churn | Regular for desktop apps that create transient WebView-backed panels, previews, or auth/content containers | [#34413](https://github.com/dotnet/maui/issues/34413) | [#34413](https://github.com/dotnet/maui/issues/34413) |


## Triage Matrix

These populated triage fields are inferred from the tracker proof, repro branch, source path, and platform notes. Treat them as a first-pass prioritization aid; exact fix risk and workaround quality should be revisited when a specific fix is designed.

Retained MiB values in this matrix are synthetic proof payloads. They demonstrate that a leak can retain realistic-sized objects, but they are not directly comparable across repros because each repro chooses payload size and iteration count to isolate a specific root. Use the MiB value as proof scale only, then prioritize by root lifetime, trigger likelihood, affected surface, native/resource pressure, and workaround quality.

Priority grades are first-pass triage urgency. GREF/JavaObject pressure and natural high-volume triggers such as scrolling, virtualization, image churn, and navigation loops intentionally raise priority; synthetic retained MiB values do not directly determine the grade.

Priority distribution: P0=0, P1=66, P2=150, P3=27, P4=2, P5=2.

| ID | Priority | Impact / proof evidence | Root lifetime | Recoverability | Workaround quality | Fix scope / risk | Likely user symptom |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| C016 | P1 | Synthetic proof scale: retained about 125 MiB; not directly comparable across repros. Prioritize with root lifetime, trigger likelihood, affected surface, and native/resource involvement. | Shared publisher/event-source root; controls/pages stay reachable through strong event or command subscriptions. | Recovered by clearing/replacing the shared publisher or unsubscribing before the subscriber should die. | Public workaround: clear the property, unsubscribe, or avoid long-lived shared publishers before disposing/navigating away. | Image/media result ownership; medium-high risk due native resource disposal contracts. | Pages/view models fail to collect after navigation or source reuse; heap grows silently. |
| C020 | P1 | Synthetic proof scale: retained about 80 MiB plus native/resource pressure; not directly comparable across repros. Prioritize with root lifetime, trigger likelihood, affected surface, and native/resource involvement. | Live owner/logical-tree/resource-listener root; removed items stay reachable from the live owner or app-held removed item. | Recovered by removing items individually, clearing parent/listener state, disconnecting handlers, or releasing the owner. | Public workaround: stop animations/tickers before disposing owners; framework should still make disposal idempotently stop native roots. | Animation platform service; medium risk, ensure stop/dispose semantics remain compatible. | Android managed heap/GREF/native-peer growth; may surface as OOM or stale activity retention. |
